# To-do prompts index

Generated 2026-11-10 from 2532 open Todoist tasks. One prompt file per task, grouped by category.
Run a category as one session: open Claude Code on this repo and say "work through todo-prompts/<category>/, oldest first, and write results to the PARA homes named in each file".

## Counts by category

| Category | Tasks | PARA home |
|---|---|---|
| [Health and medical](health-medical/) | 428 | `20_Areas/Health` |
| [Faith and character](faith-character/) | 100 | `20_Areas/Spirituality` |
| [Relationships and personal](relationships-personal/) | 192 | `20_Areas/Relationships-and-Associations` |
| [Home, property and studio](home-property/) | 269 | `20_Areas/Toop-Street-Property or 20_Areas/Morley-Studio` |
| [IT, devices, accounts and storage](it-devices-storage/) | 571 | `20_Areas/IT-Devices-and-Storage` |
| [AI, agents, tools and automation](ai-agents-automation/) | 268 | `10_Projects/2026-11_second-brain-system or 30_Resources/Prompts` |
| [Marketing, content and creative](marketing-content/) | 243 | `20_Areas/Hydralada-Marketing or 10_Projects/<campaign>` |
| [Finance, subscriptions and admin](finance-admin/) | 32 | `20_Areas/Finance-and-Subscriptions` |
